  99: 
 100: def evertse_prime_ideal_norm_bound(
 101:     rational_primes: Sequence[int], field_degree: int
 102: ) -> int:
 103:     """Bound norms of prime ideals above a fixed rational-prime support."""
 104:     if field_degree < 1:
 105:         raise ValueError("field degree must be positive")
 106:     if any(prime < 2 or not is_prime(prime) for prime in rational_primes):
 107:         raise ValueError("support must contain rational primes")
 108:     return max((prime**field_degree for prime in rational_primes), default=1)
 109: 
 110: 
 111: def system_by_name(name: str) -> LinearSystem:
 112:     return next(system for system in SYSTEMS if system.name == name)
 113: 
 114: 
 115: def identity(size: int) -> Matrix:
 116:     return tuple(
 117:         tuple(1 if row == column else 0 for column in range(size))
 118:         for row in range(size)
 119:     )
 120: 
 121: 
 122: def matmul(left: Matrix, right: Matrix, modulus: int | None = None) -> Matrix:
 123:     rows = len(left)
 124:     inner = len(right)
 125:     columns = len(right[0])
 126:     entries = []
 127:     for row in range(rows):
 128:         out_row = []
 129:         for column in range(columns):
 130:             value = sum(left[row][k] * right[k][column] for k in range(inner))
 131:             out_row.append(value if modulus is None else value % modulus)
 132:         entries.append(tuple(out_row))
 133:     return tuple(entries)
 134: 
 135: 
 136: def matvec(matrix: Matrix, vector: Sequence[int]) -> tuple[int, ...]:
 137:     return tuple(
 138:         sum(matrix[row][column] * vector[column] for column in range(len(vector)))
 139:         for row in range(len(matrix))
 140:     )
 141: 
 142: 
 143: def determinant(matrix: Matrix) -> int:
 144:     size = len(matrix)
 145:     if size == 1:
 146:         return matrix[0][0]
 147:     total = 0
 148:     for column, entry in enumerate(matrix[0]):
 149:         minor = tuple(
 150:             tuple(row[j] for j in range(size) if j != column)
 151:             for row in matrix[1:]
 152:         )
 153:         total += (-1) ** column * entry * determinant(minor)
 154:     return total
 155: 
 156: 
 157: def digit_matrix(system: LinearSystem, digit: int) -> Matrix:
 158:     if not 0 <= digit <= system.max_digit:
 159:         raise ValueError(f"digit {digit} is outside {system.name}'s alphabet")
 160:     d = system.dimension
 161:     rows = [[0] * (d + 1) for _ in range(d + 1)]
 162:     rows[0][0] = 1
 163:     rows[0][1] = digit
 164:     for index in range(d - 1):
 165:         rows[index + 1][index + 2] = 1
 166:     for index, coefficient in enumerate(system.recurrence):
 167:         rows[d][index + 1] = coefficient
 168:     return tuple(tuple(row) for row in rows)
 169: 
 170: 
 171: def block_matrix(system: LinearSystem, word: Sequence[int]) -> Matrix:
 172:     matrix = identity(system.dimension + 1)
 173:     for digit in word:
 174:         matrix = matmul(digit_matrix(system, digit), matrix)
 175:     return matrix
 176: 
 177: 
 178: def weights(system: LinearSystem, count: int) -> list[int]:
 179:     values = list(system.initials)
 180:     while len(values) < count:
 181:         values.append(
 182:             sum(
 183:                 coefficient * values[-system.dimension + index]
 184:                 for index, coefficient in enumerate(system.recurrence)
 185:             )
 186:         )
 187:     return values[:count]
 188: 
 189: 
 190: def value(system: LinearSystem, word: Sequence[int]) -> int:
 191:     return sum(digit * weight for digit, weight in zip(word, weights(system, len(word))))
 192: 
 193: 
 194: def greedy_word(system: LinearSystem, number: int) -> Word:
 195:     if number <= 0:
 196:         return ()
 197:     available = list(system.initials)
 198:     while available[-1] <= number:
 199:         available.append(
 200:             sum(
 201:                 coefficient * available[-system.dimension + index]
 202:                 for index, coefficient in enumerate(system.recurrence)
 203:             )
 204:         )
 205:     if available[-1] > number:
 206:         available.pop()
 207:     digits_msd = []
 208:     remainder = number
 209:     for weight in reversed(available):
 210:         digit, remainder = divmod(remainder, weight)
 211:         if digit > system.max_digit:
 212:             raise ValueError(f"digit bound too small for {system.name}")
 213:         digits_msd.append(digit)
 214:     if remainder:
 215:         raise ValueError(f"greedy expansion failed for {system.name}")
 216:     return tuple(reversed(digits_msd))
 217: 
 218: 
 219: def is_canonical(system: LinearSystem, word: Sequence[int]) -> bool:
 220:     if not word or word[-1] == 0:
 221:         return False
 222:     if system.name == "fibonacci":
 223:         return all(left + right <= 1 for left, right in zip(word, word[1:]))
 224:     return tuple(word) == greedy_word(system, value(system, word))
 225: 
 226: 
 227: def matrix_order(matrix: Matrix, modulus: int, limit: int = 2_000_000) -> int:
 228:     if modulus < 2:
 229:         raise ValueError("modulus must be at least 2")
 230:     if gcd(determinant(matrix), modulus) != 1:
 231:         raise NonInvertibleAction(
 232:             f"determinant {determinant(matrix)} is not a unit modulo {modulus}"
 233:         )
 234:     reduced = tuple(tuple(entry % modulus for entry in row) for row in matrix)
 235:     target = identity(len(matrix))
 236:     power = target
 237:     for exponent in range(1, limit + 1):
 238:         power = matmul(reduced, power, modulus)
 239:         if power == target:
 240:             return exponent
 651:             polynomial=f"(x-{prime})(x^2-x-1)",
 652:             recurrence=(-prime, 1 - prime, prime + 1),
 653:             initials=reachable_state,
 654:             max_digit=1,
 655:         )
 656:         companion = tuple(
 657:             tuple(row[1:]) for row in digit_matrix(inflated, 0)[1:]
 658:         )
 659:         check(determinant(companion) == -prime)
 660:         check(matvec(companion, reachable_state) == reachable_next)
 661:         twice = matvec(companion, reachable_next)
 662:         check(
 663:             tuple(
 664:                 twice[index] - reachable_next[index] - reachable_state[index]
 665:                 for index in range(3)
 666:             )
 667:             == (0, 0, 0)
 668:         )

 404: def valuation(number: int, prime: int) -> int:
 405:     exponent = 0
 406:     while number % prime == 0:
 407:         number //= prime
 408:         exponent += 1
 409:     return exponent

 411: 
 412: def geometric_ratio_support_obstruction(
 413:     trailing_coefficient: int, initial_factor: int, ratio: int
 414: ) -> dict[str, int] | None:
 415:     """Return the first local-congruence obstruction to ``c * b**t``."""
 416:     if trailing_coefficient == 0 or initial_factor < 1 or ratio < 2:
 417:         raise ValueError("the tail, initial factor, and ratio must define a scheme")
 418:     for prime in prime_factors(ratio):
 419:         if trailing_coefficient % prime != 0:
 420:             exponent = valuation(initial_factor, prime)
 421:             return {
 422:                 "prime": prime,
 423:                 "modulus": prime ** (exponent + 1),
 424:                 "initial_valuation": exponent,
 425:             }
 426:     return None

 429: def has_bounded_outside_support_mcfl(system: LinearSystem) -> bool:
 430:     """Decision rule when the recurrence polynomial is minimal Perron."""
 431:     return system.dimension == 1

 434: def omega_outside(number: int, excluded_primes: Iterable[int]) -> int:
 435:     excluded = set(excluded_primes)
 436:     return sum(prime not in excluded for prime in prime_factors(number))
 437: 
 438: 
 439: def in_local_prime_layer(
 440:     number: int,
 441:     maximum_outside_primes: int,
 442:     excluded_primes: Iterable[int],
 443:     valuation_bounds: dict[int, int],
 444: ) -> bool:
 445:     excluded = tuple(excluded_primes)
 446:     return (
 447:         number >= 1
 448:         and omega_outside(number, excluded) <= maximum_outside_primes
 449:         and all(valuation(number, prime) <= valuation_bounds[prime] for prime in excluded)
 450:     )

 453: def local_layer_isolation_modulus(
 454:     number: int,
 455:     excluded_primes: Iterable[int],
 456:     valuation_bounds: dict[int, int],
 457: ) -> int:
 458:     excluded = tuple(excluded_primes)
 459:     if not in_local_prime_layer(
 460:         number, omega_outside(number, excluded), excluded, valuation_bounds
 461:     ):
 462:         raise ValueError("the point violates an excluded-prime valuation bound")
 463: 
 464:     outside = tuple(
 465:         prime for prime in prime_factors(number) if prime not in set(excluded)
 466:     )
 467:     outside_core = product_power = 1
 468:     prime_power_modulus = 1
 469:     for prime in outside:
 470:         exponent = valuation(number, prime)
 471:         product_power *= prime**exponent
 472:         prime_power_modulus *= prime ** (exponent + 1)
 473:     outside_core = product_power
 474: 
 475:     smooth_factors = [1]
 476:     for prime in excluded:
 477:         smooth_factors = [
 478:             factor * prime**exponent
 479:             for factor in smooth_factors
 480:             for exponent in range(valuation_bounds[prime] + 1)
 481:         ]
 482:     candidates = tuple(outside_core * factor for factor in smooth_factors)
 483:     differences = tuple(abs(candidate - number) for candidate in candidates if candidate != number)
 484: 
 485:     auxiliary = 2
 486:     forbidden = set(excluded) | set(outside)
 487:     while (
 488:         not is_prime(auxiliary)
 489:         or auxiliary in forbidden
 490:         or any(difference % auxiliary == 0 for difference in differences)
 491:     ):
 492:         auxiliary += 1
 493:     return prime_power_modulus * auxiliary
 494: 
 495: 
 496: def construct_deep_congruence_chain(
 497:     initial: int, specifications: Sequence[tuple[int, int]]
 498: ) -> tuple[int, ...]:
 499:     if initial < 1:
 500:         raise ValueError("the initial value must be positive")
 501:     chain = [initial]
 502:     for modulus_factor, depth in specifications:
 503:         if modulus_factor < 1 or depth < 1:
 504:             raise ValueError("modulus factors and depths must be positive")
 505:         current = chain[-1]
 506:         quotient = 1 + modulus_factor * current**depth
 507:         chain.append(current * quotient)
 508:     return tuple(chain)
 509: 
 510: 
 511: def construct_divisibility_tree(
 512:     root: int,
 513:     nodes: Sequence[tuple[int, ...]],
 514:     thresholds: dict[tuple[int, ...], int],
 515: ) -> tuple[dict[tuple[int, ...], int], dict[tuple[int, ...], int]]:
 516:     if not nodes or nodes[0] != () or root < 1:
 517:         raise ValueError("nodes must begin with the root and the root value must be positive")
 518:     values = {(): root}
 519:     edge_quotients: dict[tuple[int, ...], int] = {}
 520:     for node in nodes[1:]:
 521:         parent = node[:-1]
 522:         if parent not in values:
 523:             raise ValueError("every parent must precede its children")
 524:         threshold = thresholds[node]
 525:         small_prime_product = 1
 526:         for candidate in range(2, threshold + 1):
 527:             if is_prime(candidate):
 528:                 small_prime_product *= candidate
 529:         previous_product = root * small_prime_product
 530:         for quotient in edge_quotients.values():
 531:             previous_product *= quotient
 532:         parent_value = values[parent]
 533:         quotient = 1 + parent_value * previous_product
 534:         values[node] = parent_value * quotient
 535:         edge_quotients[node] = quotient
 536:     return values, edge_quotients

+    def test_nonintegral_weak_perron_mixed_radix_family(self):
+        for left_radix, right_radix in ((2, 3), (2, 5), (3, 5)):
+            with self.subTest(radices=(left_radix, right_radix)):
+                system = verifier.mixed_radix_weak_perron_system(
+                    left_radix, right_radix
+                )
+                product_radix = left_radix * right_radix
+                expected_weights = [
+                    product_radix ** (index // 2)
+                    * (left_radix if index % 2 else 1)
+                    for index in range(10)
+                ]
+                self.assertEqual(verifier.weights(system, 10), expected_weights)
+                self.assertEqual(system.recurrence, (product_radix, 0))
+                self.assertEqual(
+                    [
+                        verifier.value(system, (0,) * (2 * exponent) + (1,))
+                        for exponent in range(6)
+                    ],
+                    [product_radix**exponent for exponent in range(6)],
+                )
+
+    def test_increasing_selection_needs_only_distinct_positive_values(self):
+        values = (10, 5, 7, 1, 13, 2, 16)
+
+        self.assertEqual(
+            verifier.select_increasing_return(values, start_index=0, return_time=2),
+            6,
+        )
+

+        for left_radix, right_radix in ((2, 3), (2, 5), (3, 5)):
+            with self.subTest(radices=(left_radix, right_radix)):
+                system = verifier.mixed_radix_weak_perron_system(
+                    left_radix, right_radix
+                )
+                product_radix = left_radix * right_radix
+                expected_weights = [
+                    product_radix ** (index // 2)
+                    * (left_radix if index % 2 else 1)
+                    for index in range(10)
+                ]
+                self.assertEqual(verifier.weights(system, 10), expected_weights)
+                self.assertEqual(system.recurrence, (product_radix, 0))
+                self.assertEqual(
+                    [
+                        verifier.value(system, (0,) * (2 * exponent) + (1,))
+                        for exponent in range(6)
+                    ],
+                    [product_radix**exponent for exponent in range(6)],
+                )
+
+    def test_increasing_selection_needs_only_distinct_positive_values(self):
+        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verifier, "select_increasing_return"))
+        values = (10, 5, 7, 1, 9, 2, 16)
+
+        self.assertEqual(
+            verifier.select_increasing_return(values, start_index=0, return_time=2),
+            6,
+        )
+

             verifier.geometric_ratio_support_obstruction(

     return next(system for system in SYSTEMS if system.name == name)
 
 
+def mixed_radix_weak_perron_system(
+    left_radix: int, right_radix: int
+) -> LinearSystem:
+    """Return the alternating-radix system with weak-Perron root sqrt(pq)."""
+    if left_radix < 2 or right_radix < 2:
+        raise ValueError("both radices must be at least two")
+    product_radix = left_radix * right_radix
+    return LinearSystem(
+        name=f"mixed_radix_{left_radix}_{right_radix}",
+        polynomial=f"x^2-{product_radix}",
+        recurrence=(product_radix, 0),
+        initials=(1, left_radix),
+        max_digit=max(left_radix, right_radix) - 1,
+    )
+
+
+def select_increasing_return(
+    values: Sequence[int], start_index: int, return_time: int
+) -> int:
+    """Select a later return whose value exceeds the value at the start."""
+    if return_time < 1 or not 0 <= start_index < len(values):
+        raise ValueError("the start index and return time must be valid")
+    if any(value < 1 for value in values) or len(set(values)) != len(values):
+        raise ValueError("return-orbit values must be positive and pairwise distinct")
+    for index in range(start_index + return_time, len(values), return_time):
+        if values[index] > values[start_index]:
+            return index
+    raise LookupError("the finite sample contains no increasing return")
+
+
 def identity(size: int) -> Matrix:
     return tuple(
         tuple(1 if row == column else 0 for column in range(size))

     return next(system for system in SYSTEMS if system.name == name)
 
 
+def mixed_radix_weak_perron_system(
+    left_radix: int, right_radix: int
+) -> LinearSystem:
+    """Return the alternating-radix system with weak-Perron root sqrt(pq)."""
+    if left_radix < 2 or right_radix < 2:
+        raise ValueError("both radices must be at least two")
+    product_radix = left_radix * right_radix
+    return LinearSystem(
+        name=f"mixed_radix_{left_radix}_{right_radix}",
+        polynomial=f"x^2-{product_radix}",
+        recurrence=(product_radix, 0),
+        initials=(1, left_radix),
+        max_digit=max(left_radix, right_radix) - 1,
+    )
+
+
+def select_increasing_return(
+    values: Sequence[int], start_index: int, return_time: int
+) -> int:
+    """Select a later return whose value exceeds the value at the start."""
+    if return_time < 1 or not 0 <= start_index < len(values):
+        raise ValueError("the start index and return time must be valid")
+    if any(value < 1 for value in values) or len(set(values)) != len(values):
+        raise ValueError("return-orbit values must be positive and pairwise distinct")
+    for index in range(start_index + return_time, len(values), return_time):
+        if values[index] > values[start_index]:
+            return index
+    raise LookupError("the finite sample contains no increasing return")
+
+
 def identity(size: int) -> Matrix:
     return tuple(
         tuple(1 if row == column else 0 for column in range(size))

+def mixed_radix_weak_perron_system(
+    left_radix: int, right_radix: int
+) -> LinearSystem:
+    """Return the alternating-radix system with weak-Perron root sqrt(pq)."""
+    if left_radix < 2 or right_radix < 2:
+        raise ValueError("both radices must be at least two")
+    product_radix = left_radix * right_radix
+    return LinearSystem(
+        name=f"mixed_radix_{left_radix}_{right_radix}",
+        polynomial=f"x^2-{product_radix}",
+        recurrence=(product_radix, 0),
+        initials=(1, left_radix),
+        max_digit=max(left_radix, right_radix) - 1,
+    )
+
+
+def select_increasing_return(
+    values: Sequence[int], start_index: int, return_time: int
+) -> int:
+    """Select a later return whose value exceeds the value at the start."""
+    if return_time < 1 or not 0 <= start_index < len(values):
+        raise ValueError("the start index and return time must be valid")
+    if any(value < 1 for value in values) or len(set(values)) != len(values):
+        raise ValueError("return-orbit values must be positive and pairwise distinct")
+    for index in range(start_index + return_time, len(values), return_time):
+        if values[index] > values[start_index]:
+            return index
+    raise LookupError("the finite sample contains no increasing return")
+
+
 def identity(size: int) -> Matrix:
     return tuple(
         tuple(1 if row == column else 0 for column in range(size))

+        max_digit=max(left_radix, right_radix) - 1,
+    )
+
+
+def select_increasing_return(
+    values: Sequence[int], start_index: int, return_time: int
+) -> int:
+    """Select a later return whose value exceeds the value at the start."""
+    if return_time < 1 or not 0 <= start_index < len(values):
+        raise ValueError("the start index and return time must be valid")
+    if any(value < 1 for value in values) or len(set(values)) != len(values):
+        raise ValueError("return-orbit values must be positive and pairwise distinct")
+    for index in range(start_index + return_time, len(values), return_time):
+        if values[index] > values[start_index]:
+            return index
+    raise LookupError("the finite sample contains no increasing return")
+

     return tuple(
         tuple(1 if row == column else 0 for column in range(size))
